# Skills · F · part 8

870 entries, feature-branch-protocol-template to feature-planning. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **feature-branch-protocol-template**: A template for building your own work-discipline skill — a repeatable shape for starting any meaningful piece of NSLS work, checking in as you go, and handing off cleanly. Not a ready-to-use protocol. Copy this file into your own personal skills folder (`~/.claude/skills/&lt;your-protocol-name&gt;/SKILL.md`), then fill in each section with your own version. Red's developer-flavored version is included as a seed example so you can see the shape before you make it yours. ([thensls/nsls-builder-toolkit](https://github.com/thensls/nsls-builder-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/feature-branch-protocol-template))
- **feature-branch-workflow**: Use to ship a change end-to-end on a feature branch: create the branch, run a code review on the working changes, then commit with a descriptive Conventional Commits message that links the work-item ID, push, and open a PR. Trigger whenever the user wants to "commit my changes", "open a PR", "create a feature branch", "ship this", "raise a pull request", mentions an Azure DevOps work item / story / task ID (e.g. #1234) to attach to a commit, or asks for a review before committing — even if they only name one step, prefer running the full branch → review → commit → push → PR flow. ([Amira-Samir/AIFeeds](https://github.com/Amira-Samir/AIFeeds/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/feature-branch-workflow))
